Remarks.&mdash;During July the highest reading of the barometer was 30.050, on the 12th; and the lowest 29.760, on the 26th. The highest temperature was 9^.5, on the 12th ; the lowest 740, on the&#39; 15th and 16th ; and thewnean during month was 84&deg;.5. S.E. winds prevailed, and the strongest was recorded on the 25th, averaging 12.5 miles an hour during 24 hours. Rain fell on 19 days, measuring 6.03 inches. During August, the highest reading of the barometer \vas 3o.-io8, on the 29th; and the lowest 29.608, 011 the 4th. The highest temperature was 990, on the 4th and 5tli^*&quot;the lowest 740, on the 15th; and the mean during month was 84&deg;.5. S.E. winds prevailed, and the strongest was recorded 011 the 26th, averaging 12.1 miles an hour during 24 hours. Rain fell on 18 days,&quot;measuring 14.09 inches. During September the highest reading of the barometer was 30.100, on the 12th; and tlft lowest 29.670, on the 25th. The highest temperature was 96&deg;, on the 18th; the lowest 750, 011 the 30th; and the mean during month was 83&deg;.7. N.E. winds prevailed, and the strongest was recorded on the 30th, averaging 24.7 miles an hour during 24 hours. Rain.fell on 18 days, measuring 5.07 inches. &bull; &bull; IX.
